$ cd /vercel/share/work/brief05 && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import re, html, hashlib
h = open('raw.html', encoding='utf-8').read()
start = h.find('<div class="context_message"')
gt = h.find('>', start) + 1
# find matching closing div for context_message
depth, i = 1, gt
tag = re.compile(r'<(/?)div\b[^>]*>', re.I)
for m in tag.finditer(h, gt):
    depth += -1 if m.group(1) else 1
    if depth == 0:
        end = m.start(); break
inner = h[gt:end]
tags = sorted(set(re.findall(r'<\s*/?\s*([a-zA-Z0-9]+)', inner)))
print('tags in body:', tags)
open('inner.html','w',encoding='utf-8').write(inner)
# convert: each <div>...</div> is one line; <br> inside empty div = blank line
s = inner
s = re.sub(r'<div><br\s*/?></div>', '\n', s, flags=re.I)
s = re.sub(r'<br\s*/?>', '\n', s, flags=re.I)
s = re.sub(r'</div>\s*<div[^>]*>', '\n', s, flags=re.I)
s = re.sub(r'</?div[^>]*>', '\n', s, flags=re.I)
# keep other inline tags' text only (after reporting them)
s = re.sub(r'<(?!/?(?:div|br)\b)[^>]+>', '', s)
s = html.unescape(s).replace('\r', '').replace('\u00a0', ' ')
s = re.sub(r'\n{3,}', '\n\n', s).strip() + '\n'
open('brief05.txt','w',encoding='utf-8').write(s)
print('chars', len(s), 'lines', s.count('\n'))
print('sha256', hashlib.sha256(s.encode()).hexdigest())
print('placeholders:', sorted(set(re.findall(r'<[^<>\n]{1,60}>', s)))[:40])
EOF

$ cd /vercel/share/work/brief05 && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import re, html, hashlib
inner = open('inner.html', encoding='utf-8').read()
parts = re.findall(r'<div[^>]*>(.*?)</div>', inner, flags=re.S | re.I)
leftover = re.sub(r'<div[^>]*>.*?</div>', '', inner, flags=re.S | re.I).strip()
print('div lines', len(parts), '| text outside divs:', repr(leftover[:200]))
lines = []
for p in parts:
    if re.fullmatch(r'\s*<br\s*/?>\s*', p, flags=re.I):
        lines.append('')
    else:
        t = re.sub(r'<br\s*/?>', '\n', p, flags=re.I)
        t = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '', t)
        lines.append(html.unescape(t).replace('\u00a0', ' ').rstrip())
s = '\n'.join(lines).strip('\n') + '\n'
open('brief05.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(s)
print('chars', len(s), 'lines', s.count('\n'), 'sha256', hashlib.sha256(s.encode()).hexdigest())
EOF

--- Inspected raw capture schema ---
$ cd /vercel/share/v0-project/invitation-research && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import json
def walk(o, p='', depth=0, out=None):
    if isinstance(o, dict):
        for k, v in o.items():
            walk(v, f'{p}.{k}' if p else k, depth+1, out)
    elif isinstance(o, list):
        out.append(f'{p}: list[{len(o)}]' + (f' e.g. {json.dumps(o[0])[:90]}' if o else ''))
    else:
        out.append(f'{p}: {json.dumps(o)[:90]}')
for f in ['raw/wevitation/C-WEV-104.json']:
    d = json.load(open(f)); out = []
    walk(d, out=out)
    print('=====', f, len(out), 'leaves')
    print('\n'.join(l for l in out if not l.startswith(('structure.afterOpen.headings','structure.beforeOpen.headings'))))
EOF

--- Surveyed capture field coverage ---
$ cd /vercel/share/v0-project/invitation-research && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import json, glob, collections, os
paths = sorted(glob.glob('raw/*/C-*.json'))
def leaves(o, p=''):
    if isinstance(o, dict):
        for k, v in o.items():
            yield from leaves(v, f'{p}.{k}' if p else k)
    else:
        yield p
freq = collections.Counter()
for f in paths:
    d = json.load(open(f))
    for l in set(x for x in leaves(d) if not x.startswith(('rea.','files.','structure.afterOpen.libs.','structure.afterOpen.features.','structure.beforeOpen.features.','network.byType.','lighthouse.byType.','lighthouse.throttling','lighthouse.screen','lighthouse.scores','lighthouse.metrics'))):
        freq[l] += 1
print('== key frequency (of', len(paths), ')')
for k, c in sorted(freq.items()):
    if c < len(paths): print(f'{c:3} {k}')
print('== always present:', sorted(k for k, c in freq.items() if c == len(paths)))
rows = []
for f in paths:
    d = json.load(open(f)); s = d.get('structure') or {}; lh = d.get('lighthouse') or {}
    b = (s.get('beforeOpen') or {}).get('features') or {}; a = (s.get('afterOpen') or {}).get('features') or {}
    rows.append((d['id'], d.get('kind'), d.get('driver','-'), (d.get('method') or '')[:25], lh.get('ok'), (lh.get('error') or lh.get('runtimeError') or '')[:30] if isinstance(lh.get('error') or lh.get('runtimeError'), str) else '', 'LHbytes' if lh.get('totalBytes') is not None else 'noLHbytes', s.get('coverClicked'), s.get('coverClickMethod'), s.get('coverClickLanding'), 'before' if 'beforeOpen' in s else 'nobefore', b.get('coverGate'), a.get('coverGate'), (d.get('robots') or {}).get('note','')[:28]))
for r in rows: print(' | '.join(str(x) for x in r))
EOF

import json, glob, collections, statistics
paths = sorted(glob.glob('raw/*/C-*.json'))
c = collections.Counter(); nobf = []
for f in paths:
    d = json.load(open(f)); lh = d.get('lighthouse') or {}; s = d.get('structure') or {}
    th = lh.get('throttling') or {}; sc = lh.get('screen') or {}
    c[(lh.get('lighthouseVersion'), th.get('cpuSlowdownMultiplier'), th.get('rttMs'), sc.get('width'), sc.get('mobile'))] += 1
    if 'features' not in (s.get('beforeOpen') or {}): nobf.append(d['id'])
print('LH method combos:', dict(c))
print('captures without beforeOpen.features:', nobf)
def load(i, comp):
    return json.load(open(f'raw/{comp}/{i}.json'))
wev = [load(f'C-WEV-10{i}', 'wevitation') for i in range(1, 7)]
inv5 = load('C-INV-005', 'invitesion')
med = lambda xs: statistics.median(xs)
print('WEV perf', [w['lighthouse']['scores']['performance'] for w in wev], '-> median', med([w['lighthouse']['scores']['performance'] for w in wev]))
print('WEV lcp s', med([w['lighthouse']['metrics']['lcpMs'] for w in wev]) / 1000)
print('WEV tbt ms', med([w['lighthouse']['metrics']['tbtMs'] for w in wev]))
lhk = [w['lighthouse']['totalBytes'] / 1024 for w in wev]
print('WEV LH KiB', [round(x) for x in lhk], '-> median', round(med(lhk), 1))
cdpk = [w['network']['bytes'] / 1024 for w in wev]
print('WEV CDP KiB', [round(x) for x in cdpk], '-> median', round(med(cdpk), 1))
i5 = inv5['lighthouse']['totalBytes'] / 1024
print('C-INV-005 LH KiB', round(i5, 1), 'perf', inv5['lighthouse']['scores']['performance'], 'lcp', inv5['lighthouse']['metrics']['lcpMs'], 'tbt', inv5['lighthouse']['metrics']['tbtMs'], 'cls', inv5['lighthouse']['metrics']['cls'], '| ratio', round(med(lhk) / i5, 2))
print('C-INV-005 method:', inv5.get('method','')[:200])
print('C-INV-005 extra keys:', [k for k in inv5 if k not in ('structure','network','lighthouse','rea','files')])
print({k: inv5.get(k) for k in ('note','notes','limitations','build','variant') if k in inv5})
EOF

--- Verified dossier comparison ratios ---
$ cd /vercel/share/v0-project/invitation-research/_tools/capture && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import eligibility as e
caps = e.load_captures(); by = {c['id']: c for c in caps}
reg = e.load_register(known_ids=set(by)); res = e.evaluate_all(caps, reg)
def med(comp, fn, family='coldload_eligible'):
    s = [(res[c['id']]['theme_key'], c) for c in caps if c['competitor'] == comp and res[c['id']][family]]
    return e.theme_median(s, fn)
LH = lambda c: c['lighthouse']['totalBytes'] / 1024
CDP = lambda c: c['network']['bytes'] / 1024
own = {i: by[i] for i in ('C-INV-001', 'C-INV-002', 'C-INV-005')}
for i, c in own.items():
    l = c['lighthouse']; print(i, 'driver', c.get('driver', 'pre-s06'), '| LH KiB', round(LH(c), 1), 'LH req', l['requests'], '| CDP KiB', round(CDP(c), 1), 'CDP req', c['network']['requests'], '| perf', l['scores']['performance'], 'lcp', round(l['metrics']['lcpMs']), 'tbt', round(l['metrics']['tbtMs']), 'cls', l['metrics']['cls'], 'a11y', l['scores']['accessibility'])
for comp in ('enviory', 'invitato', 'katsudoto', 'wevitation', 'invi', 'viding', 'barunson'):
    lh, n, k = med(comp, LH); cdp_s, ns, ks = med(comp, CDP, 'structure_eligible'); cdp_c, nc, kc = med(comp, CDP)
    p = med(comp, lambda c: c['lighthouse']['scores']['performance'])[0]; lcp = med(comp, lambda c: c['lighthouse']['metrics']['lcpMs'])[0]
    tbt = med(comp, lambda c: c['lighthouse']['metrics']['tbtMs'])[0]; cls = med(comp, lambda c: c['lighthouse']['metrics']['cls'])[0]
    a11y = med(comp, lambda c: c['lighthouse']['scores']['accessibility'])[0]
    print(f"{comp:10} themes {n} caps {k} | LH KiB {lh:,.1f} ratio vs C-INV-005 {lh / LH(own['C-INV-005']):.2f} | CDP KiB (cold-load-eligible set) {cdp_c:,.1f} ratio vs C-INV-001 CDP {cdp_c / CDP(own['C-INV-001']):.2f} | CDP structure-eligible {cdp_s:,.1f} | perf {p} lcp {lcp/1000:.2f}s tbt {tbt:.0f} cls {cls:.3f} a11y {a11y}")
EOF

$ cd /vercel/share/v0-project/invitation-research/_tools/safety && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import glob, json, importlib.util, os, re
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('r', 'redact-demo-names.py'); r = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(r)
hits = {}
for p in sorted(glob.glob('../../raw/*/C-*.json')):
    s = json.load(open(p))
    if not r.in_scope(s): continue
    st = s.get('structure') or {}; a = st.get('afterOpen') or {}; b = st.get('beforeOpen') or {}
    title_words = r.name_words(a.get('title'))
    texts = [x.get('heading') for x in a.get('sections') or []] + list(a.get('buttons') or []) + list(b.get('buttons') or [])
    found = set()
    for t in texts:
        if isinstance(t, str) and '&' in t:
            w = r.name_words(t)
            if w: found.add(tuple(sorted(w)))
    if found:
        hits[s['id']] = (len(found), 'title-derived=' + str(bool(title_words)))
print(len(hits), 'in-scope captures with an "X & Y" name pair outside the title:')
for k, v in hits.items(): print(' ', k, v)
EOF

lines = [l.strip() for l in text.split('\n') if l.strip()]
print('text lines:', len(lines)); print(' | '.join(lines[:220])[:5000])

import re, html
def text(fn):
    h = open(fn, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read()
    t = re.sub(r'<script.*?</script>|<style.*?</style>', ' ', h, flags=re.S)
    return h, [l.strip() for l in html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '\n', t)).split('\n') if l.strip()]
for fn in ('undangan-digital.html', 'template-design.html', 'buku-tamu-digital.html'):
    h, lines = text(fn)
    print(f'\n===== {fn}: {len(lines)} lines; Rp mentions:', len(re.findall(r'Rp\.?\s?[\d.,]+', h)), sorted(set(re.findall(r'Rp\.?\s?[\d.,]+[KkJj]?', h)))[:30])
    if fn == 'template-design.html':
        ids = sorted(set(int(x) for x in re.findall(r'theme/preview/(\d+)', h)))
        studio = sorted(set(int(x) for x in re.findall(r'studio\.viding\.co/theme/preview/(\d+)', h)))
        print('preview ids on catalog:', len(ids), ids[:60], '| studio ids:', studio[:40])
        cats = [l for l in lines if re.search(r'(?i)kategori|category|filter|premium|basic|free|gratis|exclusive|signature', l)]
        print('category-ish lines:', cats[:40])
    print(' | '.join(lines[:160])[:3500])
EOF

$ B=/vercel/share/work/invitesion-private/pages/demo-search && cd $B && UA="Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 14; Pixel 8) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/156.0.0.0 Mobile Safari/537.36" && get() { printf '%s ' "$(date -u +%H:%M:%SZ)"; curl -sS -m 40 -A "$UA" -H "Accept-Language: id-ID,id;q=0.9,en;q=0.6" -o "$2" -w "$1 http=%{http_code} bytes=%{size_download} final=%{url_effective}\n" "$1"; sleep 2; } && get https://indoinvite.com/contoh/undangan-online-video idi-contoh-video.html && get https://indoinvite.com/contoh/undangan-jasa-desain idi-contoh-jasa.html && get https://satumomen.com/tema smo-tema.html && get https://paleo.id/ plo-home.html && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import re, html, collections
def analyse(fn, host):
    h = open(fn, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read()
    title = re.search(r'<title>(.*?)</title>', h, re.S)
    hrefs = re.findall(r'href="([^"#]+)"', h)
    shapes = collections.Counter()
    for u in hrefs:
        u2 = re.sub(r'^https?://(www\.)?' + re.escape(host), '', u)
        if u2.startswith('http'): shapes['external:' + re.sub(r'^https?://([^/]+).*', r'\1', u2)] += 1; continue
        m = re.match(r'^/([^/?]+)', u2); shapes['/' + (m.group(1) if m else '')] += 1
    cf = 'cf-chl' in h or 'challenge-platform' in h or 'Just a moment' in h
    text = re.sub(r'<script.*?</script>|<style.*?</style>', ' ', h, flags=re.S)
    lines = [l.strip() for l in html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '\n', text)).split('\n') if l.strip()]
    demo_words = [l for l in lines if re.search(r'(?i)\b(demo|contoh|preview|lihat tema|lihat contoh|coba)\b', l)][:15]
    print(f'\n=== {fn}: title={html.unescape(title.group(1)).strip()[:90] if title else None} | challenge={cf} | links={len(hrefs)} | text lines={len(lines)}')
    print('link shapes:', dict(shapes.most_common(18)))
    print('demo-ish lines:', demo_words)
    return h, lines
analyse('idi-contoh-video.html', 'indoinvite.com')
analyse('idi-contoh-jasa.html', 'indoinvite.com')
h, L = analyse('smo-tema.html', 'satumomen.com')
print('SMO first lines:', ' | '.join(L[:60])[:1500])
h, L = analyse('plo-home.html', 'paleo.id')
print('PLO links:', sorted(set(re.findall(r'href="([^"#]+)"', h)))[:20])
print('PLO first lines:', ' | '.join(L[:50])[:1200])
EOF

import re, collections
g = open('idi-contoh-video.html', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read()
idx = [m.start() for m in re.finditer(r'>\s*Preview\s*<', g)]
print('Preview buttons:', len(idx))

for i in idx[:40]:
    tag_start = g.rfind('<', 0, i)
    tag = g[tag_start:i+1]
    attrs = re.findall(r'([a-zA-Z-]+)="([^"]*)"', tag)
    norm = []
    for k, v in attrs:
        if k in ('href', 'data-src', 'data-url', 'onclick', 'data-video', 'data-href'):
            v2 = re.sub(r'https?://([^/]+)', r'<\1>', v)
            v2 = re.sub(r'/[A-Za-z0-9_-]{6,}', '/<slug>', v2)
            v2 = re.sub(r'\d+', 'N', v2)

        else:

    shapes[' '.join(norm)] += 1
for s, c in shapes.most_common(6): print(c, '×', s)

    text = [t.strip() for t in html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '\n', re.sub(r'<script.*?</script>', ' ', chunk, flags=re.S))).split('\n') if t.strip()]
    cards.append((m.group(1), text[-12:]))
print('cards:', len(cards))
words = collections.Counter()
for slug, t in cards: words.update(w for w in t if len(w) < 30)
print('frequent card words:', words.most_common(25))
for slug, t in cards[:12]: print(slug, '|', ' / '.join(t[-8:])[:180])
EOF

import re, html, hashlib, importlib.util, glob
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('r', '/vercel/share/v0-project/invitation-research/_tools/safety/redact-demo-names.py'); r = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(r)
H = lambda s: hashlib.sha256(s.lower().encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
pairs = {}
for s in ('cream-flowers', 'phinisi-pink', 'java-maroon-3d'):
    h = open(f'smo-prev-{s}.html', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read()
    title = re.search(r'<title>(.*?)</title>', h, re.S); title = html.unescape(title.group(1)).strip() if title else ''
    text = html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', re.sub(r'<script.*?</script>|<style.*?</style>', ' ', h, flags=re.S)))
    words = r.name_words(title)
    amp = [t for t in re.findall(r"([A-Z][A-Za-z'’]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’]+)?\s*&\s*[A-Z][A-Za-z'’]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'’]+)?)", text)][:6]
    phones = len(re.findall(r'(?:\+62|\b08)\d[\d\s-]{7,12}\d', text))
    accts = len(re.findall(r'\b\d{10,16}\b', text))
    generic = sorted(set(w for w in re.findall(r'(?i)\b(nama mempelai|nama pria|nama wanita|romeo|juliet|contoh|demo|sample|placeholder|lorem ipsum)\b', text)))
    pairs[s] = sorted(H(w) for w in words)
    print(f"{s}: title words hashed={pairs[s]} | generic markers={generic} | '&' pairs hashed={[H(a) for a in amp]} | phone-like={phones} | 10-16 digit runs={accts} | chars={len(text)}")
vals = list(pairs.values())
print('same title name pair across all three themes:', all(v == vals[0] for v in vals) and bool(vals[0]))
EOF

import re, html
h = open('harga.html', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read()
text = re.sub(r'<script.*?</script>|<style.*?</style>', ' ', h, flags=re.S)
L = [l.strip() for l in html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '\n', text)).split('\n') if l.strip()]
pk = [i for i in range(len(L) - 2) if L[i + 1] == 'Rp.' and re.match(r'^[\d.]+$', L[i + 2])]
for i in pk:
    name, sub, price = L[i - 1], L[i], L[i + 2]
    nxt = pk[pk.index(i) + 1] if pk.index(i) + 1 < len(pk) else i + 60
    block = L[i + 3: min(nxt - 1, i + 60)]
    incl = [b for b in block if not b.startswith('(tidak') and b not in ('Tampilkan Lebih Banyak',)]
    excl = [block[k - 1] for k, b in enumerate(block) if b.startswith('(tidak')]
    print(f'{name} | {sub} | Rp {price} | included({len(incl)}): {"; ".join(incl[:22])[:420]} | excluded({len(excl)}): {"; ".join(excl[:14])[:260]}')
tabs = [l for l in L if l in ('Satuan', 'Berlangganan')]; print('tabs:', tabs)
sub = [l for l in L if re.search(r'(?i)berlangganan|/bulan|per bulan|reseller|undangan/bulan', l)][:12]; print('subscription lines:', sub)

$ cd /vercel/share/work/invitesion-private/pages/indoinvite && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import re, html
from collections import Counter, OrderedDict
h = open('harga.html', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read()
cards = re.split(r'class="card shadow card-pricelist', h)[1:]
print('cards:', len(cards))
allfeat = OrderedDict()
rows = []
fills = Counter()
for c in cards:
    name = re.search(r'font-size: 2em; font-weight:600">\s*(.*?)\s*</p>', c, re.S)
    sub = re.search(r'fst-italic text-center">\s*(.*?)\s*</p>', c, re.S)
    price = re.search(r'Rp\.</span>\s*([\d.]+)', c)
    strike = re.findall(r'<(?:del|s|strike)[^>]*>(.*?)</(?:del|s|strike)>', c, re.S)
    feats = []
    for m in re.finditer(r'<div class="d-flex align-items-center mb-3">(.*?)</div>\s*</div>', c, re.S):
        block = m.group(1)
        fill = re.search(r'fill="(#[0-9A-Fa-f]{3,6})"', block)
        txt = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', block); txt = html.unescape(re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', txt)).strip()
        f = fill.group(1).upper() if fill else 'none'
        fills[f] += 1
        feats.append((f, txt))
    n = html.unescape(name.group(1)).strip() if name else '?'
    rows.append((n, html.unescape(sub.group(1)).strip() if sub else '', price.group(1) if price else '?', strike, feats))
print('icon fills:', fills)
for n, s, p, st, feats in rows:
    inc = [t for f, t in feats if f == '#03BB16']
    exc = [f'{t} [{f}]' for f, t in feats if f != '#03BB16']
    print(f'\n== {n} | {s} | Rp {p} | strike={st} | rows={len(feats)}')
    print('  INCLUDED:', '; '.join(inc))
    print('  NOT INCLUDED:', '; '.join(exc))
# other page facts
text = re.sub(r'<script.*?</script>|<style.*?</style>', ' ', h, flags=re.S)
L = [l.strip() for l in html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '\n', text)).split('\n') if l.strip()]
for kw in ('Berlangganan', 'Satuan', 'diskon', 'Diskon', 'promo', 'Promo', 'garansi', 'Garansi', 'pembayaran', 'Pembayaran', 'QRIS', 'transfer', 'reseller', 'Reseller', 'WO', 'Wedding Organizer', 'masa aktif', 'Masa Aktif', 'FAQ', 'Pertanyaan'):
    hits = [l for l in L if kw in l][:4]
    if hits: print(f'\n[{kw}]', ' || '.join(x[:200] for x in hits))
print('\nheadings:', [html.unescape(re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", x)).strip()[:90] for x in re.findall(r'<h[1-3][^>]*>(.*?)</h[1-3]>', h, re.S)][:25])
EOF

$ cd /vercel/share/work/invitesion-private/pages/indoinvite && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import re, html
from collections import Counter
h = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', open('harga.html', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read())
cards = re.split(r'class="card shadow card-pricelist', h)[1:]
print('cards:', len(cards))
fills = Counter(); rows = []
for c in cards:
    c = c[:c.find('Tampilkan Lebih Banyak') + 400] if 'Tampilkan Lebih Banyak' in c else c[:20000]
    name = re.search(r'font-size: 2em; font-weight:600">\s*(.*?)\s*</p>', c)
    sub = re.search(r'fst-italic text-center">\s*(.*?)\s*</p>', c)
    price = re.search(r'Rp\.</span>\s*([\d.]+)', c)
    strike = re.findall(r'<(?:del|s|strike)\b[^>]*>(.*?)</(?:del|s|strike)>', c)
    feats = []
    for m in re.finditer(r'<div class="d-flex align-items-center mb-3">(.*?)</div> </div>', c):
        block = m.group(1)
        fill = re.search(r'fill="(#[0-9A-Fa-f]{3,6})"', block)
        txt = html.unescape(re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', block))).strip()
        f = fill.group(1).upper() if fill else 'none'
        fills[f] += 1; feats.append((f, txt))
    rows.append((html.unescape(name.group(1)) if name else '?', html.unescape(sub.group(1)) if sub else '', price.group(1) if price else '?', strike, feats))
print('icon fills:', dict(fills))
for n, s, p, st, feats in rows:
    inc = [t for f, t in feats if f == '#03BB16']
    exc = [f'{t} [{f}]' for f, t in feats if f != '#03BB16']
    print(f'\n== {n} | {s} | Rp {p} | strike={st} | rows={len(feats)} inc={len(inc)} exc={len(exc)}')
    print('  INC:', '; '.join(inc)[:900])
    print('  EXC:', '; '.join(exc)[:700])
i = h.find('Support Pembayaran'); print('\n[payment block]', re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' | ', h[i:i+2500])[:900])
for kw in ('Satuan', 'Berlangganan'):
    j = h.find('>' + kw + '<'); print(f'\n[{kw} tab ctx]', re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', h[j-300:j+300]))[:400])
EOF

import json, re
d = json.load(open('raw/indoinvite/discovery-IDI.json'))
def walk(o, p=''):
    if isinstance(o, dict):
        for k, v in o.items(): yield from walk(v, f'{p}.{k}' if p else k)
    elif isinstance(o, list):
        yield (p, f'list[{len(o)}]')
        for i, v in enumerate(o[:3]): yield from walk(v, f'{p}[{i}]')
    else: yield (p, o)
seen = 0
for p, v in walk(d):
    s = str(v)
    if len(s) > 220: s = s[:220] + '…'
    print(f'{p} = {s}'); seen += 1
    if seen > 90: break
EOF

--- Mined preserved SMO/PLO pages ---
$ cd /vercel/share/work/invitesion-private/pages/demo-search && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import re, html
from collections import Counter
for fn in ('smo-tema.html', 'plo-home.html'):
    h = open(fn, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read()
    print(f'\n===== {fn} ({len(h)} bytes) =====')
    t = re.search(r'<title>(.*?)</title>', h, re.S); print('title:', html.unescape(t.group(1)).strip() if t else None)
    m = re.search(r'<meta name="description" content="([^"]*)"', h); print('desc:', html.unescape(m.group(1))[:300] if m else None)
    links = re.findall(r'href="([^"#]+)"', h)
    internal = Counter(re.sub(r'^https?://(www\.)?(satumomen\.com|paleo\.id)', '', l) for l in links if not l.startswith(('http', '//')) or 'satumomen.com' in l or 'paleo.id' in l)
    nav = [l for l in internal if not l.startswith('/preview/') and not l.startswith('/app/create-card') and not re.search(r'\.(css|js|png|jpg|webp|svg|ico|woff2?)', l)]
    print('internal non-theme links:', sorted(nav)[:60])
    ext = sorted({re.sub(r'^(https?://[^/]+).*', r'\1', l) for l in links if l.startswith('http') and 'satumomen.com' not in l and 'paleo.id' not in l})
    print('external hosts:', ext[:30])
    play = sorted(set(re.findall(r'play\.google\.com/store/apps/details\?id=[\w.]+', h))); print('play:', play)
    for kw in ('next', '__NEXT_DATA__', '_next/static', 'nuxt', 'livewire', 'laravel', 'XSRF', 'inertia', 'react', 'vue', 'gtag', 'googletagmanager', 'fbq', 'tiktok', 'cloudflare', 'midtrans', 'xendit', 'tripay', 'duitku'):
        n = len(re.findall(re.escape(kw), h, re.I))
        if n: print(f'  {kw}: {n}')
    text = re.sub(r'<script.*?</script>|<style.*?</style>', ' ', h, flags=re.S)
    L = [l.strip() for l in html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '\n', text)).split('\n') if l.strip()]

import re, html
for fn in ('harga.html', 'reseller-undangan-digital-website.html'):

    m = re.search(r'<meta name="description" content="([^"]*)"', h); print('desc:', html.unescape(m.group(1))[:300] if m else None)
    text = re.sub(r'<script.*?</script>|<style.*?</style>|<svg.*?</svg>', ' ', h, flags=re.S)

    print('lines:', len(L))
    # skip nav: start at first line containing Rp or a heading keyword
    start = next((i for i, l in enumerate(L) if re.search(r'Rp\.?\s?\d', l)), 0)
    s0 = max(0, start - 40)
    print(' | '.join(L[s0:s0 + 260])[:7000])
    strikes = re.findall(r'<(?:del|s|strike)\b[^>]*>(.*?)</(?:del|s|strike)>', h, re.S)
    print('strikethrough:', [html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '', x)).strip() for x in strikes][:20])
    print('line-through class count:', len(re.findall(r'line-through', h)))

import re, json, html
from collections import Counter, OrderedDict
h = open('preview-1205.html', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read()
print('title:', re.search(r'<title>(.*?)</title>', h, re.S).group(1).strip() if re.search(r'<title>', h) else None)
areas = re.findall(r'"Area_Name"\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"', h)
print('Area_Name values (%d):' % len(areas), list(OrderedDict.fromkeys(areas)))
editable = re.findall(r'"Area_Name"\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"[^{}]*?"Edit_YN"\s*:\s*"([YN])"', h)
print('Area/Edit pairs:', editable[:40])
keys = Counter(re.findall(r'"([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{2,40})"\s*:', h))
print('JSON keys (top 90):', [k for k, _ in keys.most_common(90)])
yn = OrderedDict()
for k, v in re.findall(r'"([A-Za-z_]+_YN)"\s*:\s*"([YN])"', h):
    yn.setdefault(k, set()).add(v)
print('*_YN flags:', {k: sorted(v) for k, v in yn.items()})
scripts = re.findall(r'<script[^>]+src="([^"]+)"', h); print('scripts:', [s.rsplit("/", 1)[-1][:60] for s in scripts])
ko = re.findall(r'[\uac00-\ud7a3][\uac00-\ud7a3\s]{1,14}', re.sub(r'<script.*?</script>', ' ', h, flags=re.S))
print('visible Korean labels (static HTML):', list(OrderedDict.fromkeys(x.strip() for x in ko))[:60])

I'm settling the brand direction for the dashboard and marketing, keeping the rose primary, while letting each template carry its own palette and motion, with reduced-motion fallbacks. --- Extracted dossier feature sections ---
$ mkdir -p /tmp/fm && cd /vercel/share/v0-project/invitation-research/03-teardowns && python3 -B - <<'EOF'
import re, pathlib
skip = re.compile(r'^#{2,3} (4\.[123] (What was measured|Cold|After|Measurements)|5\. Tech|6\. Public API|4\.1 What was measured|4\.6 Against)')
for d in sorted(pathlib.Path('.').glob('*/dossier.md')):
    out=[]; keep=True
    for line in d.read_text().splitlines():
        if line.startswith('## ') or line.startswith('### '):
            keep = not skip.match(line)
        if keep: out.append(line)
    p = pathlib.Path('/tmp/fm')/f'{d.parent.name}.md'
    p.write_text('\n'.join(out))
    print(d.parent.name, len('\n'.join(out)))
EOF

def walk(o,p=''):
    if isinstance(o,dict):
        for k,v in o.items():
            if isinstance(v,(dict,list)) and len(json.dumps(v))>200: walk(v,p+k+'.')

--- Computed gap and lead signals ---
$ cd /vercel/share/v0-project/invitation-research/05-feature-matrix && python3 -B - <<'EOF' | tee /tmp/fm/signals.txt
import csv
rows=list(csv.DictReader(open('feature-matrix.csv')))
comps=['IDI','PLO','SMO','WEV','VID','ENV','IVT','INV','KAT','STM','BRN','PPO','RSV','JOY','PTF']
indo=['IDI','PLO','SMO','WEV','VID','ENV','IVT','INV','KAT']
gaps=[];leads=[]
for r in rows:
    full=[c for c in comps if r[c]=='Full']; known=[c for c in comps if r[c]!='Unknown']
    fi=[c for c in indo if r[c]=='Full']
    if r['invitesion'] in ('None','Partial') and len(full)>=3:
        gaps.append((len(fi),len(full),r['feature_id'],r['invitesion'][0],r['feature'],','.join(full)))
    if r['invitesion']=='Full' and known and sum(1 for c in known if r[c]!='Full')>=max(2,len(known)//2):
        leads.append((r['feature_id'],r['feature'],'not-Full:'+','.join(c+'='+r[c][0] for c in known if r[c]!='Full')))
gaps.sort(reverse=True)
print('GAPS (ours None/Partial, >=3 competitors Full) count',len(gaps))
for g in gaps: print(f"{g[2]} ours={g[3]} indoFull={g[0]} allFull={g[1]} | {g[4]} | {g[5]}")
print('\nLEADS (ours Full, majority of known competitor cells not Full) count',len(leads))
for l in leads: print(' | '.join(l))
EOF

    if r['priority'] not in ('P0','P1','P2') or r['effort'] not in 'SML' or r['risk'] not in 'LMH': errs.append((r['gap_id'],'enum'))
from collections import Counter
print(len(rows),'rows; errors:',errs or 'none'); print(Counter(r['priority'] for r in rows), Counter(r['gap_type'] for r in rows))

import re
src=open('lib/db/schema.ts').read()
for t in ['invitations','guests','mediaAssets','guestMessages','analyticsEvents']:
    m=re.search(r'export const '+t+r' = pgTable\(\s*\'(\w+)\',\s*\{(.*?)\n\s*\}', src, re.S)
    if not m: print('NOTFOUND',t); continue
    cols=re.findall(r"(\w+):\s*(\w+)\('(\w+)'", m.group(2))
    print(m.group(1), '->', ', '.join(f"{c[2]}:{c[1]}" for c in cols))
EOF

--- Thought for 2s ---
I'm fixing the borrow errors by binding the parameter arrays to locals and scoping the futures in a block, so the borrows end before the client is dropped.


Splitting the query setup into the rest of the block and dropping the client once the futures finish, since the early return still works inside a block expression. I also need the ToSql import, and running three queries through try_join! on one client should be fine because tokio-postgres pipelines them on a single connection.


The borrow-checker errors come from query parameter temporaries outliving the joined futures, so I'm binding them to locals and scoping the futures to end the borrow before the client returns to the pool.
